LOD streaming implementation contract closure audit
Executable: fnv_reverse/FalloutNV.exe
Fallout New Vegas 1.4.0.525, PE32 x86, image base 0x00400000
SHA256: 42fee7d6cd74e801372aa89c8f71c974cebd3c20ec9ad43d1465b8fa9646b49c
Identity reconfirmed against the supplied executable before this research.
Primary interface: callable radare2 MCP.
Evidence: verbatim instruction, xref and data results captured in this research.
Interpretation and implementation boundaries:
  docs/lod_streaming_handoff_engine_fix_plan.md
Existing Ghidra authoritative output was not regenerated or changed.
Function discovery can merge adjacent functions or show a containing function.
Use instruction addresses and verified entry/return spans, not inferred tool names.
Direct xrefs do not establish exhaustive indirect or external-provider coverage.
The cell +0xC0 getter is a worldspace getter, not a scene-node getter.
Worldspace +0x3C is inherited LODdata; 0x00929260 writes its +0x28 byte.
0x006FEDE0 tests node overlap with the active cell grid.
No real-game execution, timings, memory comparison or FPS result was produced.
